\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}+\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}+\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}.

By Assumption 3 and Proposition
8
,
\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\|{\mathbf{R}}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
and
\mathbb{E}[\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}]=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(pm_{i}T^{2}\right)
. In addition, by Lemma 8 in
8
,
\mathbb{E}[\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}]=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(p^{2}m_{i}T+pm_{i}^{2}T^{2}\right)
. Therefore, we obtain bounds of the last three terms of
\frac{1}{T}\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}
,

\left\|\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{m_{i}}T}\right),

\left\|\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{m_{i}}T}\right),

and

\left\|\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{m_{i}}T^{3/2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}T}\right).

In other words,

\left\|\frac{1}{T}\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}-\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{m_{i}}T}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}T}\right).

On the other hand, by Assumption 3 and Proposition
7
, we obtain

\left\|\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}-\frac{1}{p}{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}=\mathbf{o}_{p}\left(1\right).

Together, we have

\left\|\frac{1}{T}\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}-\frac{1}{p}{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}\left(1\right).

By the inequality that for the
j
-th largest eigenvalue of any square matrices
{\mathbf{A}}
and
\widehat{{\mathbf{A}}}
,
|\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}})-\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{A})|\leq\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{A}\|_{2}
, as
m_{i},p,T\to\infty
, we have

\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*},

where the eigenvalues of
\frac{1}{p}\mathbf{R}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*}\mathbf{R}^{\top}
and
\frac{1}{p}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\mathbf{R}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*}
are the same,
\frac{1}{p}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\mathbf{R}\to\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}
by Assumption 3, and the eigenvalues of
\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*}
and
(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}
are also the same since
\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}
and
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*}
are both positive definite. Furthermore, the top
k
eigenvalues of
\frac{1}{T}\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}
are bounded away from zero and infinity, and therefore we obtain
\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
and
\|(\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
.
∎

Lemma 4
.

Under Assumptions 3-4 and 7-9 with known
k
and
r
, for any
i\in[s_{1}]
and
j\in[s_{2}]
, we have
\|{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
and
\|{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}}^{*}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
.

Proof.

We only prove the result for
\|{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\|_{2}
, since the proof for
\|{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}}^{*}\|_{2}
is similar.
Recall that

{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*}=\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}.

By Proposition
7
, as
p,m_{i},T\to\infty
, we have

\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*}.

Moreover,
\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}{\mathbf{R}}
and
(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}
are bounded according to Assumption 3 and Proposition
9
, while the estimator
\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}
is also bounded. Therefore, the final results are derived.
∎

Proposition 10
.

Under Assumptions 3-4 and 7-9 with known
k
and
r
, for any
i\in[s_{1}]
and
j\in[s_{2}]
, as
m_{i},n_{j},T\to\infty
, we have

\frac{\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}}{p}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\quad\text{and}\quad\frac{\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}}{q}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}}^{*}.

The matrices
{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}=({\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}(\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{-1/2}
and
{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}}^{*}=({\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}}^{*})^{1/2}(\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*})^{\top}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FR}_{j}}^{*})^{-1/2}
, where the matrices
\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}
and
\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*}
are diagonal matrices of eigenvalues (ordered decreasingly) of
(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}
and
(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FR}_{j}}^{*})^{1/2}\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{C}}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FR}_{j}}^{*})^{1/2}
, respectively, with the corresponding eigenvectors
\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}
and
\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*}
satisfying
(\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}=\mathbf{I}
and
(\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*})^{\top}\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*}=\mathbf{I}
. Here
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*}=\int_{0}^{1}\widetilde{{\mathbf{W}}}(u)\bm{\Omega}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{i}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{W}}}(u)^{\top}\text{d}u
and
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FR}_{j}}^{*}=\int_{0}^{1}\widetilde{{\mathbf{W}}}(u)^{\top}\bm{\Omega}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{W}}}(u)\text{d}u
, where
\widetilde{{\mathbf{W}}}(u)={\mathbf{W}}(u)+\frac{\widetilde{\alpha}}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{W}}(t/T)
and
{\mathbf{W}}(\cdot)
is an
k\times r
matrix of Brownian motions with the covariance of
\text{vec}({\mathbf{W}}(\cdot))
being
\overline{{\mathbf{G}}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{u}}\overline{{\mathbf{G}}}^{\top}
.

Proof.

We only prove the conclusion for
\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}
, since the proof of results for
\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}
is similar. According to Equation (2.5) and the definition of
\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}
in Section 4, we have

\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}=\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{Y}}}_{it}\widetilde{{\mathbf{Y}}}_{it}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}.

By left-multiplying
\frac{1}{p}(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top})^{1/2}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}
on the both sides and expanding
\widetilde{{\mathbf{Y}}}_{it}
as
\widetilde{{\mathbf{Y}}}_{it}={\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}+\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}
, we have

{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}=\left[{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{*}+{\mathbf{d}}_{i}^{*}({\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*})^{-1}\right]{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*},

(A4)

where
k\times k
matrices
{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{*}
,
{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*}
, and
{\mathbf{d}}_{i}^{*}
are defined as

{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{*}=\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}\right)^{1/2}\frac{{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}}{p}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}\right)^{1/2},

{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*}=\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}\right)^{1/2}\frac{{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}}{p},

and

\displaystyle{\mathbf{d}}_{i}^{*}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}\right)^{1/2}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\left[\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}\right.

\displaystyle\ \ \ \left.+\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}+\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}\right)^{1/2}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\left(\mathrm{I}_{1}+\mathrm{I}_{2}+\mathrm{I}_{3}\right)\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}.

According to Assumption 3 and Proposition
7
, we have

\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}\right)^{1/2}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1).

Following Proposition
9
, we have
\|\mathrm{I}_{1}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}\left(1\right)
,
\|\mathrm{I}_{2}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}\left(1\right)
, and
\|\mathrm{I}_{3}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}\left(1\right)
. Moreover,
\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
. Together, we have

\|{\mathbf{d}}^{*}_{i}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}\left(1\right).

According to Proposition
9
, as
p,m_{i},T\to\infty
, we have

({\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*}=\frac{\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}}{p}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}\right)\frac{{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}}{p}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}\frac{1}{pT}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{M}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}=\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}.

In other words, the eigenvalues of
({\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*}
are not close to zero nor infinity, and therefore
\|({\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*})^{-1}\|_{2}=\|({\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*})^{-1}[({\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}\|_{2}^{1/2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
.

Let
{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{B}}_{i}}^{*}
denote the diagonal matrix consisting of diagonal elements of
({\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*}
, satisfying
{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{B}}_{i}}^{*}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}
, as
\underline{m},p,T\to\infty
. Denote the normalized
k\times k
matrix
\bm{\Psi}_{{\mathbf{B}}_{i}}^{*}={\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*}({\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{B}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1/2}
. Then,
\|\bm{\Psi}_{{\mathbf{B}}_{i}}^{*}\|_{2}=\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}{[({\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{B}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}({\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*}]}}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}1
. According to Equation (
A4
), we have

\bm{\Psi}_{{\mathbf{B}}_{i}}^{*}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}=\left[{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{*}+{\mathbf{d}}_{i}^{*}({\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*})^{-1}\right]\bm{\Psi}_{{\mathbf{B}}_{i}}^{*}.

Since
\|{\mathbf{d}}^{*}_{i}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}\left(1\right)
and
\|({\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*})^{-1}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
, by Assumption 3 and Proposition
7
, as
\underline{m},p,T\to\infty
, we have

{\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{*}+{\mathbf{d}}_{i}^{*}({\mathbf{B}}_{i}^{*})^{-1}={\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{*}+\mathbf{o}_{p}\left(1\right)\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}.

According to the eigenvector perturbation theory (
17
,
17
) and Assumption 9, there exists a unique orthogonal eigenvector matrix
\Psi_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}
of
(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}
, such that

\left\|\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}-\bm{\Psi}_{{\mathbf{B}}_{i}}^{*}\right\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}(1).

Finally, we complete our proof as

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbf{R}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}{p}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}\right)^{-1/2}\bm{\Psi}_{{\mathbf{B}}_{i}}^{*}({\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{B}}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{-1/2}\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}(\mathbf{V}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}=({\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}.

∎

Proposition 11
.

Under Assumptions 3-4 and 7-9 with known
k
and
r
, for any
i\in[s_{1}]
and
j\in[s_{2}]
, as
m_{i},n_{j},T\to\infty
, we have

{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}({\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}\quad\text{and}\quad{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}}^{*}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}({\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}}^{*})^{-1}.

The matrices
{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}=({\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}(\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{-1/2}
and
{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}}^{*}=({\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}}^{*})^{1/2}(\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*})^{\top}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FR}_{j}}^{*})^{-1/2}
, where the matrices
\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}
and
\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*}
are diagonal matrices of eigenvalues (ordered decreasingly) of
(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}
and
(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FR}_{j}}^{*})^{1/2}\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{C}}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FR}_{j}}^{*})^{1/2}
, respectively, with the corresponding eigenvectors
\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}
and
\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*}
satisfying
(\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}=\mathbf{I}
and
(\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*})^{\top}\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*}=\mathbf{I}
. Here
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*}=\int_{0}^{1}\widetilde{{\mathbf{W}}}(u)\bm{\Omega}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{i}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{W}}}(u)^{\top}\text{d}u
and
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FR}_{j}}^{*}=\int_{0}^{1}\widetilde{{\mathbf{W}}}(u)^{\top}\bm{\Omega}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{W}}}(u)\text{d}u
, where
\widetilde{{\mathbf{W}}}(u)={\mathbf{W}}(u)+\frac{\widetilde{\alpha}}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{W}}(t/T)
and
{\mathbf{W}}(\cdot)
is an
k\times r
matrix of Brownian motions with the covariance of
\text{vec}({\mathbf{W}}(\cdot))
being
\overline{{\mathbf{G}}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{u}}\overline{{\mathbf{G}}}^{\top}
.

Proof.

We only prove the conclusion for
\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}
, since proof of results for
\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*}
is similar. By the definition of
\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}
in Section 4, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*}({\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}({{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}=(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathbf{FC}_{i}}^{*})^{1/2}\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}(\mathbf{V}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1/2}=({\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1},

where the second step follows from Lemma B.3. in
31
and Propositions
9
-
10
, and the last step follows from
(\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\bm{\Psi}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}=\mathbf{I}
.
∎

Lemma 5
.

Under Assumptions 3-4 and 7-9 with known
k
and
r
, for any
i\in[s_{1}]\text{ and }j\in[s_{2}]
, we have

\frac{1}{p}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{RH}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p^{2}T^{2}}\right),\quad\text{as }m_{i},p,T\to\infty,

and

\frac{1}{q}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}-\mathbf{CH}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}}^{*}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{n_{j}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{q^{2}T^{2}}\right),\quad\text{as }n_{j},q,T\to\infty.

For any
l_{1}\in[p]
and
l_{2}\in[q]
, we have

\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}^{l_{1}}-\mathbf{R}^{l_{1}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p^{2}T^{2}}\right),\quad\text{as }m_{i},p,T\to\infty,

and

\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{l_{2}}-\mathbf{C}^{l_{2}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{n_{j}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{q^{2}T^{2}}\right),\quad\text{as }n_{j},q,T\to\infty.

Proof.

We only prove the conclusion for
\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}
, since the proof of the results for
\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}_{j}-{\mathbf{C}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}}^{*}
is similar. From the estimation procedure of
\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}
and the definition of
\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}
in Section 4, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{Y}}_{it}\widetilde{\mathbf{Y}}_{it}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}-\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}+\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}\right)\left({\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}+\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}\right)^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}

\displaystyle\ \ \ -\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}+\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}+\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\right)\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{I}_{1}+\mathrm{I}_{2}+\mathrm{I}_{3}.

According to Assumption 3 and Propositions
8
-
9
,
\text{as }m_{i},p,T\to\infty
, we have,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\left\|\mathrm{I}_{1}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p}\left\|\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\frac{\|{\mathbf{R}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}{p}\frac{\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\frac{\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}{p}\left\|(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\left\|\mathrm{I}_{2}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p}\left\|\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\frac{\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\frac{\|{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}{p}\frac{\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}{p}\left\|(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\right).

Since
{\mathbf{E}}_{t}
is stationary and
\alpha
-mixing, by Lemma B.2. in
40
, as
m_{i},p,T\to\infty
, we have
\frac{1}{p}\|\frac{1}{pm_{i}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(\frac{1}{p^{2}}+\frac{1}{pm_{i}T})+\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)\times\frac{1}{p}\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{RH}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}
. Accordingly, we have

\frac{1}{p}\left\|\mathrm{I}_{3}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{p^{2}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{3}}\right)+\mathbf{o}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right)\times\frac{1}{p}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{RH}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}.

Together, as
m_{i},p,T\to\infty
, we have

\frac{1}{p}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{RH}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p^{2}T^{2}}\right).

In addition, we consider the
l_{1}
-th row vector,

\displaystyle\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}^{l_{1}}-{\mathbf{R}}^{l_{1}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbf{R}}^{l_{1}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}+\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{l_{1}}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}+\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{l_{1}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\right)\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}_{1}+\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}_{2}+\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}_{3}.

According to Assumption 3 and Propositions
8
-
9
,
\text{as }m_{i},p,T\to\infty
, we have,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}_{1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{R}}^{l_{1}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\|{\mathbf{R}}^{l_{1}}\|_{2}^{2}\frac{\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\frac{\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}}{p}\left\|(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\left\|\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}_{2}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{l_{1}}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\frac{\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{l_{1}}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{m_{i}T^{2}}\frac{\|{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\|_{2}^{2}}{p}\frac{\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}}{p}\left\|(\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}\right),

where Proposition
8
implies that

\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{l_{1}}{\mathbf{C}}_{i}\widetilde{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\top}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}({{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}+\alpha\overline{{\mathbf{F}}})\Big[\sum_{\tau=1}^{m_{i}}({\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\tau})^{\top}e_{it,l_{1}\tau}\Big]\right\|_{2}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left({m_{i}T^{2}}\right).

Moreover, Equation (B.4) in
40
implies that
\|\frac{1}{pm_{i}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{l_{1}}\widetilde{{\mathbf{E}}}_{it}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(\frac{1}{p^{2}}+\frac{1}{pm_{i}T})+\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)\times\frac{1}{p}\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{RH}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}
. Accordingly, we have

\left\|\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}_{3}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{p^{2}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{pm_{i}T^{3}}\right)+\mathbf{o}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right)\times\frac{1}{p}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{RH}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

Together, for any
l_{1}\in[p]
, as
m_{i},p,T\to\infty
, we have

\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}^{l_{1}}-\mathbf{R}^{l_{1}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p^{2}T^{2}}\right).

∎

We next derive asymptotic properties of the global estimators and the associated auxiliary matrices.

Proposition 12
.

Under Assumptions 3-4 and 7-9 with known
k
and
r
, as
\underline{m},\underline{n},T\to\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{R}}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*}\quad\text{and}\quad\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{C}}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*},

with
\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{R}}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
,
\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
,
\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
, and
\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
. Here
\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*}
and
\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*}
are diagonal matrices of eigenvalues (ordered decreasingly) of
(\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{R}}}^{*})^{1/2}\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{R}}}^{*})^{1/2}
and
(\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{C}}}^{*})^{1/2}\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{C}}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{C}}}^{*})^{1/2}
, respectively, with
\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{R}}}^{*}=\frac{1}{s_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}[(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}
and
\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{C}}}^{*}=\frac{1}{s_{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{2}}(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*})^{-1}[(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{C}_{j}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}
.

Proof.

We only prove the conclusion for
\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}
, since the proof of results for
\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{C}}
is similar. According to Equation (2.6) and
\frac{1}{p}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}={\mathbf{I}}
, we have

\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{R}}=\frac{1}{p}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}=\frac{1}{p}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\left(\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}^{\top}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{R}}.

(A5)

Writing
\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}
as
\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}=\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}+{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}+\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}+\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}.

By Assumption 3 and Lemmas
4
-
5
, we have
\frac{1}{p}\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(\frac{1}{m_{i}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p^{2}T^{2}})
and
\frac{1}{p}\|\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
, for any
i\in[s_{1}]
. We then bound the first three terms of
\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}}
as

\left\|\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\underline{m}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p^{2}T^{2}}\right),

\left\|\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{m}}T}+\frac{1}{pT}\right),

and

\left\|\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{m}}T}+\frac{1}{pT}\right).

In other words,

\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}}-\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{m}}T}+\frac{1}{pT}\right).

On the other hand, according to Proposition
11
, we have
\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}
and thus
\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}[(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}
, as
m_{i},p,T\to\infty
, for any
i\in[s_{1}]
. Then, by Assumption 3, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}-\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}\mathbf{R}(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}[(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{s_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}-(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}[(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}\right\|_{2}\frac{1}{p}\left\|\mathbf{R}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{o}_{p}\left(1\right).

Together, we have

\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}}-\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}\mathbf{R}(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}[(\mathbf{Q}_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}}-\frac{1}{p}\mathbf{R}\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{R}}}^{*}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}\left(1\right).

By the inequality that for the
j
-th largest eigenvalue of any square matrices
{\mathbf{A}}
and
\widehat{{\mathbf{A}}}
,
|\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}})-\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{A})|\leq\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{A}\|_{2}
, as
\underline{m},p,T\to\infty
, we have

\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{R}}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*},

where the eigenvalues of
\frac{1}{p}\mathbf{R}\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{R}}}^{*}\mathbf{R}^{\top}
and
\frac{1}{p}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\mathbf{R}\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{R}}}^{*}
are the same,
\frac{1}{p}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\mathbf{R}\to\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}
by Assumption 3, and the eigenvalues of
\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{R}}}^{*}
and
(\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{R}}}^{*})^{1/2}\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{R}}}^{*})^{1/2}
are also the same since
\bm{\Omega}_{\mathbf{R}}
and
\bm{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{H_{R}}}^{*}
are both positive definite. Furthermore, the top
k
eigenvalues of
\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}}
are bounded away from zero and infinity, and therefore we have
\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{R}}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
and
\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
.
∎

Lemma 6
.

Under Assumptions 3-4 and 7-9 with known
k
and
r
, we have
\|{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{*}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
and
\|{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}^{*}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(1)
.

Proof.

We only prove the result for
\|{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{*}\|_{2}
, since the proof for
\|{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}^{*}\|_{2}
is similar.
Recall that
{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*}=\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}({\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}
. The conclusion follows directly from Assumption 3, Proposition
12
, and Lemma
4
.
∎

A.3 Proof of Theorem 1

Proof.

We only prove the conclusion for
\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}
, since the proof of results for
\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}
is similar.
As
\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times k}
is consisting of the
k
largest eigenvalues of
\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}}
(2.6), we have,

\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}=\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}=\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}.

Writing
\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}
as
\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}=\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}+{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}
, we obtain

\begin{split}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}&=\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}})(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}})^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}\\
&\ \ \ +\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}})^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}\\
&\ \ \ +\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}){\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}\\
&=\mathrm{I}_{1}+\mathrm{I}_{2}+\mathrm{I}_{3}.\end{split}

(A6)

Next, we bound
\mathrm{I}_{1}
,
\mathrm{I}_{2}
, and
\mathrm{I}_{3}
. We observe that
\mathrm{I}_{1}
is dominated by
\mathrm{I}_{2}
and
\mathrm{I}_{3}
, and
\|\mathrm{I}_{2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\asymp\|\mathrm{I}_{3}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
. For
\mathrm{I}_{2}
, by Assumption 3, Propositions
1
-
2
and
4
, we have

\frac{1}{p}\|\mathrm{I}_{2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\leq\frac{C}{s_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}\frac{\|{\mathbf{R}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}{p}\|{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\frac{\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}{p}\frac{\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}{p}\|\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\underline{m}T}+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\right),

where
C
is a positive constant. Thus, as
\underline{m},p,T\to\infty
,

\frac{1}{p}\left\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\underline{m}T}+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\right).

∎

A.4 Proof of Theorem 2

Proof.

From the estimation procedure (2.10), we have

\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}=\frac{1}{pq}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}=\frac{1}{pq}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}+\frac{1}{pq}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}.

Writing
\mathbf{R}=\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}+\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}
and
\mathbf{C}={\mathbf{C}}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1}+\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{pq}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1})\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{C}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1})^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1})\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1})^{\top}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{q}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{C}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1})^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}+\frac{1}{pq}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}.

We further decompose
\widehat{\mathbf{R}}=\widehat{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{RH}_{\mathbf{R}}+\mathbf{RH}_{\mathbf{R}}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{C}}=\widehat{\mathbf{C}}-\mathbf{CH}_{\mathbf{C}}+\mathbf{CH}_{\mathbf{C}}
in the last term of the above equation and rearrange it. We have

\begin{split}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1})^{\top}&=\frac{1}{pq}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1})\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{C}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1})^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\\
&\ \ \ +\frac{1}{p}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}(\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1})\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1})^{\top}+\frac{1}{q}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{C}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1})^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\\
&\ \ \ +\frac{1}{pq}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}-\mathbf{CH}_{\mathbf{C}})+\frac{1}{pq}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{CH}_{\mathbf{C}}\\
&\ \ \ +\frac{1}{pq}{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}-\mathbf{C}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}})+\frac{1}{pq}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}\\
&=\sum_{i=1}^{7}\mathrm{I}_{i}.\end{split}

(A7)

Since
\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\|\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)
and
\frac{1}{\sqrt{q}}\|\mathbf{C}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)
as
\underline{m},\underline{n},T\to\infty
by Theorem 1, term
\mathrm{I}_{1}
is dominated by
\mathrm{I}_{2}
and
\mathrm{I}_{3}
, and term
\mathrm{I}_{4}
is dominated by
\mathrm{I}_{5}
and
\mathrm{I}_{6}
. Now we bound
\mathrm{I}_{2},\mathrm{I}_{3},\mathrm{I}_{5},\mathrm{I}_{6}
, and
\mathrm{I}_{7}
.

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{I}_{2}\|_{\text{2}}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{p}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}})^{\top}(\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1})\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1})^{\top}+\frac{1}{p}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}(\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1})\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1})^{\top}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\underline{m}T}+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\right)^{1/2}\right),

by Assumptions 2-3, Lemma
1
, and Theorem 1. Similarly, adding Assumption 5, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{I}_{3}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\underline{n}T}+\frac{1}{q^{2}}\right)^{1/2}\right),\quad\|\mathrm{I}_{5}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{q}}\left(\frac{1}{\underline{m}T}+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\right)^{1/2}\right),

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{I}_{6}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\left(\frac{1}{\underline{n}T}+\frac{1}{q^{2}}\right)^{1/2}\right),\quad\|\mathrm{I}_{7}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{pq}}).

Finally, we have, as
\underline{m},\underline{n},T\to\infty
,

\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1})^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{m}T}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{n}T}}+\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{q}\right).

∎

A.5 Proof of Theorem 3

Proof.

From the estimation procedure (2.11), we have
\widehat{{\mathbf{S}}}_{t}=\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{\top}
and
{\mathbf{S}}_{t}={\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}
. Define
{\mathbf{R}}^{\text{H}}={\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}
,
{{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}}={\mathbf{C}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}
, and
{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{H}}={\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}({\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}^{-1})^{\top}
. Then,
{\mathbf{S}}_{t}={{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}}{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{\top}
. Under this definition, we have

\begin{split}\widehat{{\mathbf{S}}}_{t}-{\mathbf{S}}_{t}&=\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{\top}-{{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}}{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{\top}\\
&=(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}-{{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})(\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}})(\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}-{{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{\top}+(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}-{{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}}){{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}(\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}-{{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{\top}\\
&\ \ \ +{{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}}(\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}})(\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}-{{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{\top}+(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}-{{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})(\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}})({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{\top}\\
&\ \ \ +(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}-{{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}}){{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{\top}+{{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}}(\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}})({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{\top}+{{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}}{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}(\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}-{{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{\top}.\end{split}

Then, for any
i\in[p]
and
j\in[q]
, we have

\begin{split}\widehat{{\mathbf{S}}}_{t,ij}-{\mathbf{S}}_{t,ij}&=[\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}](\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}})[\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}]^{\top}+[\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}]{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}[\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}]^{\top}\\
&\ \ \ +({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}})[\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}]^{\top}+[\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}](\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}})[({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}]^{\top}\\
&\ \ \ +({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}})[({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}]^{\top}+({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}[\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}]^{\top}\\
&\ \ \ +[\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}]{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}[({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}]^{\top}.\end{split}

(A8)

Since
\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i}-{\mathbf{R}}^{i}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)
,
\|\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{j}-{\mathbf{C}}^{j}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)
, and
\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{-1})^{\top}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)
as
\underline{m},\underline{n},T\to\infty
by Theorems 1-2, the last three terms dominate
\widehat{{\mathbf{S}}}_{t,ij}-{\mathbf{S}}_{t,ij}
. By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}})[({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}]^{\top}\leq\|({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}\|_{2}\|\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}\|_{2}\|({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}\|_{2},

({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}[\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}]^{\top}\leq\|({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}\|_{2}\|{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}\|_{2}\|\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}\|_{2},

and

[\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}]{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}[({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}]^{\top}\leq\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}})^{i}\|_{2}\|{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}}\|_{2}\|({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}})^{j}\|_{2}.

By Assumptions 2-3, Lemma
1
, and Theorems 1-2, we have

\widehat{{\mathbf{S}}}_{t,ij}-{\mathbf{S}}_{t,ij}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{m}T}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{n}T}}+\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{q}\right).

∎

A.6 Proof of Theorem 4

Proof.

We only prove the conclusion for
\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i_{1}}-{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}
, since the proof of results for
\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{i_{2}}-{\mathbf{C}}^{i_{2}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{C}}
is similar.
First, we expand
\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i_{1}}-{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}
as Equation (
A6
),

\displaystyle\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i_{1}}-{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\ \ \ +\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\ \ \ +\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\right]\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathrm{I}+\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}+\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}\right)\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}.

According to Lemma
2
, term
\mathrm{I}
is dominated by
\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}
and
\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\sqrt{\overline{m}T}\left(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i_{1}}-{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\overline{m}T}}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\left[(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}+\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}\right]\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}+\mathbf{o}_{p}(1).

Considering term
\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}
, we expand
\mathbf{R}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}
as below,

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\overline{m}T}}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\overline{m}T}}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{l=1}^{p}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}^{\top}(\mathbf{R}^{l})^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{l}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\overline{m}T}}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{l=1}^{p}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}^{\top}(\mathbf{R}^{l})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}+\mathbf{o}_{p}(1),

where the last step follows from Theorem 1. Next, we transform term
\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}
to match its dominant part with the above one of
\mathrm{I}\hskip-1.2pt\mathrm{I}
as

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\overline{m}T}}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\overline{m}T}}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{l}-\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{l}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\overline{m}T}}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{l=1}^{p}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{l}-\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}^{\top}(\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}})^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{l}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\overline{m}T}}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{l=1}^{p}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{l}-\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}^{\top}(\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}+\mathbf{o}_{p}(1),

where
\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{l}-\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}
, therefore the second equality above holds, i.e.,
\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{l}-\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}=(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{l}-\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}^{\top}(\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}})^{\top}
, and the last step follows from Theorem 1. Combining the above two equations, we deduce the following results:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\overline{m}T}\left(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i_{1}}-{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\overline{m}T}}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{l=1}^{p}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}^{\top}(\mathbf{R}^{l})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{\sqrt{\overline{m}T}}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{l=1}^{p}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{l}-\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}^{\top}(\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}+\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\overline{m}T}}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle l=1}}{{l\neq i_{1}}}}^{p}\left[(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})(\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}+(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{l}-\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})(\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}\right]{\mathbf{R}}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{2\sqrt{\overline{m}T}}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})(\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}+\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle l=1}}{{l\neq{i_{1}}}}}^{p}\sqrt{m_{j}T}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{m}}{m_{j}}}\left[(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})(\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}+(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{l}-\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})(\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \times{\mathbf{R}}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}+\frac{2}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\sqrt{m_{j}T}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{m}}{m_{j}}}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})(\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}+\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\sqrt{m_{j}T}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{l}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}}){\mathbf{G}}_{{i_{1}},p,q,T}^{j,l}+\mathbf{o}_{p}(1),

where
{\mathbf{G}}_{{i_{1}},p,q,T}^{j,l}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times k}
are defined as

{\mathbf{G}}_{{i_{1}},p,q,T}^{j,l}=\begin{cases}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{m}}{m_{j}}}(\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}+\sqrt{\frac{\overline{m}}{m_{j}}}\sum_{\tau=1}^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{\tau}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\tau}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1},\quad&l={i_{1}},\\
\sqrt{\frac{\overline{m}}{m_{j}}}(\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1},\ &l\neq{i_{1}}.\end{cases}

As
\underline{m},p,T\to\infty
, by Propositions
3
(c),
4
, and
6
, we have the following asymptotic properties:

{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}^{-1},\quad\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{R}}},\quad{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1},\quad\forall j\in[s_{1}].

Then, we can derive asymptotic behavior of
{\mathbf{G}}_{i_{1},p,q,T}^{j,l}
, as
\underline{m},p,T\to\infty
,

{\mathbf{G}}_{{i_{1}},p,q,T}^{j,l}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{\mathbf{G}}_{i_{1}}^{j,l},

where

{\mathbf{G}}_{i_{1}}^{j,l}=\begin{cases}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{m}}{m_{j}}}(\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}^{-1})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}+\sqrt{\frac{\overline{m}}{m_{j}}}\sum_{\tau=1}^{p}(\mathbf{R}^{\tau}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}^{-1})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\tau}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1},&\quad l={i_{1}},\\
\sqrt{\frac{\overline{m}}{m_{j}}}(\mathbf{R}^{i_{1}}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}^{-1})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{l}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{-1},&\quad l\neq{i_{1}}.\end{cases}

As
\overline{m}/\underline{m}=\mathcal{O}(1)
,
{\mathbf{G}}_{i_{1}}^{j,l}
is bounded for any
j\in[s_{1}]\text{ and }l\in[p]
. Thus, according to Lemma
3
and Slutsky’s theorem, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{\overline{m}T}\left(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i_{1}}-{\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\sqrt{m_{j}T}({\mathbf{G}}_{{i_{1}},p,q,T}^{j,l})^{\top}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{l}_{j}-\mathbf{R}^{l}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}_{j}})^{\top}+\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\overset{\mathcal{D}}{\longrightarrow}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{\Sigma}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i_{1}}}\right),

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i_{1}}}=\mathbf{\Sigma}_{{\mathbf{R}},{\mathbf{G}}_{i_{1}}^{\top}}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p^{2}s_{1}^{2}}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{s_{1}}\sum_{l_{1}=1}^{p}\sum_{l_{2}=1}^{p}({\mathbf{G}}_{i_{1}}^{j_{1},l_{1}})^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j_{1}}}^{-1}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j_{1}}}\left(\mathbf{\Phi}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j_{1},j_{2},l_{1},l_{2}}}^{1,1}+\alpha\mathbf{\Phi}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j_{1},j_{2},l_{1},l_{2}}}^{1,2}\overline{{\mathbf{F}}}^{\top}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\ \ \ +\alpha\overline{{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{\Phi}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j_{1},j_{2},l_{1},l_{2}}}^{2,1}+\alpha^{2}\overline{{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{\Phi}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j_{1},j_{2},l_{1},l_{2}}}^{2,2}\overline{{\mathbf{F}}}^{\top}\right){\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j_{2}}}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j_{2}}}^{-1}{\mathbf{G}}_{i_{1}}^{j_{2},l_{2}}.

∎

A.7 Proof of Theorem 5

Proof.

We only prove the conclusion for
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i_{1}}}
, since proof of results for
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{2}}}
is similar. According to Propositions
1
and
3
(c) and Theorems 3.3 and 3.5 in
40
,

\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{j,t}(\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}_{j}^{*})^{\top}(\widehat{{\mathbf{E}}}_{jt}^{l})^{\top}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}^{-1}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}({\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{*}}^{-1})^{\top}({\mathbf{C}}_{j}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{*}})^{\top}({{\mathbf{E}}}_{jt}^{l})^{\top}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\top}({\mathbf{E}}_{jt}^{l})^{\top},

and similarly

\overline{\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}}_{j}(\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}_{j}^{*})^{\top}(\widehat{{\mathbf{E}}}_{jt}^{l})^{\top}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}\overline{{\mathbf{F}}}{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\top}({\mathbf{E}}_{jt}^{l})^{\top},

where
\frac{1}{m_{j}}\|\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}_{j}^{*}-{\mathbf{C}}_{j}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{*}}\|_{2}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(\frac{1}{pT}+\frac{1}{m_{j}^{2}})
,
\|\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{j,t}-{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}^{-1}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}({\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{*}}^{-1})^{\top}\|_{2}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(\frac{1}{pT}+\frac{1}{m_{j}T}+\frac{1}{pm_{j}})
, and
\widehat{{\mathbf{E}}}_{jt,l\tau}-{{\mathbf{E}}}_{jt,l\tau}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{pT}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{m_{j}T}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{pm_{j}}})
, for any
j\in[s_{1}]
,
l\in[p]
, and
\tau\in[q]
, as
m_{j},p,T\to\infty
. Then, the HAC estimator
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j_{1},j_{2},l_{1},l_{2}}}
is a consistent estimator of
{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j_{1},j_{2},l_{1},l_{2}}}
by Theorem 2 in
33
, as
\underline{m},\underline{n},T\to\infty
.

Next, by Propositions
1
,
3
(a) and (c),
4
and
6
, we have
\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}
,

\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}
,
and

(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{j}^{i_{1}})^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{l}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}({\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{l}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}({\mathbf{R}}^{i_{1}}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}^{-1})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{l}{\mathbf{Q}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}.

Therefore, for any
j\in[s_{1}]
and
l\in[p]
, as
\underline{m},\underline{n},T\to\infty
, we have

\widehat{{\mathbf{G}}}_{i_{1}}^{j,l}\overset{\mathcal{P}}{\longrightarrow}{{\mathbf{G}}}_{i_{1}}^{j,l}.

Thus, by Slutsky’s theorem,
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i_{1}}}
is a consistent estimator of
{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i_{1}}}
.
∎

A.8 Proof of Theorem 6

Proof.

We only prove the conclusion for
\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}
, since the proof of results for
\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}
is similar.
Following Equation (
A6
), we have,

\begin{split}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*}&=\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}\\
&\ \ \ +\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}\\
&\ \ \ +\frac{1}{ps_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}(\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})({\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}\\
&=\mathrm{I}_{1}+\mathrm{I}_{2}+\mathrm{I}_{3}.\end{split}

Next, we bound
\mathrm{I}_{1}
,
\mathrm{I}_{2}
, and
\mathrm{I}_{3}
. We observe that
\mathrm{I}_{1}
is dominated by
\mathrm{I}_{2}
and
\mathrm{I}_{3}
, and
\|\mathrm{I}_{2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\asymp\|\mathrm{I}_{3}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
. For
\mathrm{I}_{2}
, by Assumption 3, Proposition
12
and Lemmas
4
and
5
, we have

\frac{1}{p}\|\mathrm{I}_{2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\leq\frac{C}{s_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{s_{1}}\frac{\|{\mathbf{R}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}{p}\|{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\frac{\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}_{i}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}_{i}}^{*}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}{p}\frac{\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}{p}\|\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\underline{m}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p^{2}T^{2}}\right),

where
C
is a positive constant. Thus, as
\underline{m},p,T\to\infty
,

\frac{1}{p}\left\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}-{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*}\right\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\underline{m}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p^{2}T^{2}}\right).

∎

A.9 Proof of Theorem 7

Proof.

Following Equation (
A7
), we have,

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}[(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{pq}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}[\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{-1}]\mathbf{F}_{t}[\mathbf{C}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{1}{p}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}[\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{-1}]\mathbf{F}_{t}[(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{1}{q}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}[\mathbf{C}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{1}{pq}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}-\mathbf{CH}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*})+\frac{1}{pq}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{1}{pq}({\mathbf{H}}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}-\mathbf{C}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*})+\frac{1}{pq}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{CH}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{7}\mathrm{I}_{i}.

Since
\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\|\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{-1}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)
and
\frac{1}{\sqrt{q}}\|\mathbf{C}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*})^{-1}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)
as
\underline{m},\underline{n},T\to\infty
by Theorem 6, term
\mathrm{I}_{1}
is dominated by
\mathrm{I}_{2}
and
\mathrm{I}_{3}
, and term
\mathrm{I}_{4}
is dominated by
\mathrm{I}_{6}
and
\mathrm{I}_{7}
. Therefore we bound
\mathrm{I}_{2}
,
\mathrm{I}_{3}
,
\mathrm{I}_{5}
,
\mathrm{I}_{6}
, and
\mathrm{I}_{7}
as:

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{I}_{2}\|_{\text{2}}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{p}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{\top}[\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{-1}]\mathbf{F}_{t}[(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}\right.

\displaystyle\ \ \ \left.+\frac{1}{p}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{R}^{\top}[\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{-1}]\mathbf{F}_{t}[(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{m}}T}+\frac{1}{pT}\right),

by Assumptions 3 and 7, Lemma
6
, and Theorem 6. Similarly, adding Assumption 8(c), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{I}_{3}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{n}}T}+\frac{1}{qT}\right),\quad\|\mathrm{I}_{5}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{pq}}\right),

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{I}_{6}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{n}}T}+\frac{1}{qT}\right)\right),\quad\|\mathrm{I}_{7}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{q}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{m}}T}+\frac{1}{pT}\right)\right).

Finally, as
\underline{m},\underline{n},T\to\infty
, we have

\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{R}}^{*})^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}[(\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{C}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{m}}T}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{n}}T}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{pq}}\right).

∎

A.10 Proof of Theorem 8

Proof.

Let
{\mathbf{R}}^{\text{H}*}={\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{*}
,
{{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*}={\mathbf{C}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}^{*}
, and
{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{H}*}=({\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{*})^{-1}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}[({\mathbf{H}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}^{*})^{-1}]^{\top}
. Following Equation (
A8
), for any
i\in[p]
and
j\in[q]
, we have,

\displaystyle\widehat{{\mathbf{S}}}_{t,ij}-{\mathbf{S}}_{t,ij}

\displaystyle=[\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}](\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*})[\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}]^{\top}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +[\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}]{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*}[\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}]^{\top}+({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*})[\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}]^{\top}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +[\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}](\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*})[({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}]^{\top}+({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*})[({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}]^{\top}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*}[\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}]^{\top}+[\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}]{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*}[({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}]^{\top}.

Since
\|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)
,
\|\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)
, and
\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*}\|_{2}=\mathbf{o}_{p}(1)
by Theorems 6-7, the last three terms dominate
\widehat{{\mathbf{S}}}_{t,ij}-{\mathbf{S}}_{t,ij}
. By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we bound these terms as

({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}(\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*})[({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}]^{\top}\leq\|({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}\|_{2}\|\widehat{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}-{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*}\|_{2}\|({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}\|_{2},

({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*}[\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}]^{\top}\leq\|({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}\|_{2}\|{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*}\|_{2}\|\widehat{{\mathbf{C}}}^{j}-({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}\|_{2},

and

[\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}]{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*}[({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}]^{\top}\leq\|\widehat{{\mathbf{R}}}^{i}-({{\mathbf{R}}}^{\text{H}*})^{i}\|_{2}\|{{\mathbf{F}}}_{t}^{\text{H}*}\|_{2}\|({{\mathbf{C}}}^{\text{H}*})^{j}\|_{2}.

According to Assumptions 3 and 7, Lemma
6
, and Theorems 6-7, we then have

\widehat{{\mathbf{S}}}_{t,ij}-{\mathbf{S}}_{t,ij}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{m}}T}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\underline{n}}T}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{pq}}\right).

∎

:
Boxplots of the average and maximum column space distances
\mathcal{D}_{1}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{i})
,

,

, and
\mathcal{D}_{2}(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j})
, defined in Equation (5.3). Data are generated from model (2.1),
{\mathbf{Y}}_{t}={\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{E}}_{t}
, where
{\mathbf{F}}_{t}
and
{\mathbf{E}}_{t}
follow the AR(1) processes in Equation (5.2) with
(k,r)=(3,3)
and
\psi=0.5
, and entries of
{\mathbf{R}}
and
{\mathbf{C}}
are drawn independently from
\mathcal{U}(-1,1)
. We consider four dimension settings,
(p,q)\in\{(50,50),(50,100),(100,50),(100,100)\}
, and four sample size settings,
T\in\{0.5pq,1.0pq,1.5pq,2.0pq\}
. Results are based on 200 repetitions, with
\alpha=0
,
s_{1}=s_{2}=5
, and even partitions.

{\mathbf{Y}}_{t}={\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{E}}_{t}
, where

and
{\mathbf{E}}_{t}
follow the matrix normal distributions in Equation (5.4) with

and
(p,q)=(200,200)
, and entries of
{\mathbf{R}}
and
{\mathbf{C}}
are drawn independently from
\mathcal{U}(-1,1)
. We consider four sample size settings,

. Results are based on 1000 repetitions, with
\alpha=0
,
s_{1}=s_{2}=3
, and even partitions.

This table reports the short and full names of countries and expenditure items in the CPI dataset. The dataset contains 27 expenditure items for 52 countries over 1343 months. The observations are sorted by GDP rankings along the nation dimension (World Bank, December 16, 2024) and by variance along the item dimension in decreasing order.
